  om merkle import MerkleTree and from merkle import MerkleTreeBuilder work.

  But wait, I also have a merkle.py file at the root. In Python, if both exi
  st, the package wins. So I should either:

  1. Move merkle.py contents into the merkle/ package and delete merkle.py
  2. Or keep merkle.py and make merkle/init.py re-export from it

  update init.py.

  erkle/ exists, import merkle imports the package. But import merkle from t
  he same directory... no, the package always wins.

  ree and MerkleTreeBuilder classes, and update merkle/__init__.py to export
  them.

  │ 326 +                                                                  │

  │ 328 +                                                                  │
  │ 329 +     def write_proofs(self, output_path: Path) -> None:           │
  │ 330 +         """                                                      │
  │ 331 +         Write inclusion proofs for all leaves to JSONL file.     │
  │ 332 +                                                                  │

  │ 350 +                                                                  │
  │ 351 +     Args:                                                        │
  │ 352 +         proof: Inclusion proof dictionary                        │
  │ 353 +                                                                  │

  │ 355 +         True if proof is valid, False otherwise                  │

  │ 377 +                 + bytes.fromhex(current_hash)                    │

  │ 379 +                                                                  │

  │ 381 +                                                                  │
  │ 382 +     # Check if we reached the correct root                       │
  │ 383 +     return current_hash == proof["root_hash"]                    │

• Used WriteFile (orthogonal-engineering/threshold_loader.py)
  ╭─  +77 /home/idor/orthogonal-engineering/threshold_loader.py  ──────────╮
  │  1 + """                                                               │
  │  2 + Threshold configuration loader.                                   │
  │  3 +                                                                   │
  │  4 + Reads YAML threshold configs and CLI overrides, returning Fracti… │
  │  5 + """                                                               │
  │  6 +                                                                   │
  │  7 + from fractions import Fraction                                    │
  │  8 + from pathlib import Path                                          │
  │  9 + from typing import Dict, List, Optional                           │
  │ 10 +                                                                   │
  │ 11 +                                                                   │
  │ 12 + def _parse_fraction(value: str) -> Fraction:                      │
  │ 13 +     """Parse a rational string (e.g., '247/1' or '3/2') into a F… │
  │ 14 +     value = value.strip()                                         │
  │ 15 +     if "/" in value:                                              │
  │ 16 +         num, den = value.split("/", 1)                            │
  │ 17 +         return Fraction(int(num.strip()), int(den.strip()))       │
  │ 18 +     return Fraction(int(value), 1)                                │
  │ 19 +                                                                   │
  │ 20 +                                                                   │
  │ 21 + def load_thresholds(                                              │
  │ 22 +     path: Optional[str] = None,                                   │
  │ 23 +     overrides: Optional[List[str]] = None,                        │
  │ 24 + ) -> Dict[str, Fraction]:                                         │
  │ 25 +     """                                                           │
  │ 26 +     Load thresholds from config file and apply CLI overrides.     │
  │ 27 +                                                                   │
  │ 28 +     Precedence: overrides > config file > hard-coded defaults.    │
  │ 29 +                                                                   │
  │ 30 +     Args:                                                         │
  │ 31 +         path: Path to YAML config file.                           │
  │ 32 +         overrides: List of "key=value" strings from CLI.          │
  │ 33 +                                                                   │
  │ 34 +     Returns:                                                      │
  │ 35 +         Dictionary mapping threshold names to Fraction values.    │
  │ 36 +     """                                                           │
  │ 37 +     defaults = {                                                  │
  │ 38 +         "certain": Fraction(247, 1),                              │
  │ 39 +         "high_confidence": Fraction(200, 1),                      │
  │ 40 +         "probable": Fraction(150, 1),                             │
  │ 41 +         "unknown": Fraction(100, 1),                              │
  │ 42 +         "suspicious": Fraction(50, 1),                            │
  │ 43 +         "invalid": Fraction(0, 1),                                │
  │ 44 +     }                                                             │
  │ 45 +                                                                   │
  │ 46 +     result = dict(defaults)                                       │
  │ 47 +                                                                   │
  │ 48 +     if path:                                                      │
  │ 49 +         config_path = Path(path)                                  │
  │ 50 +         if config_path.exists():                                  │
  │ 51 +             try:                                                  │
  │ 52 +                 import yaml                                       │
  │ 53 +                                                                   │
  │ 54 +                 with open(config_path, "r") as f:                 │
  │ 55 +                     data = yaml.safe_load(f)                      │
  │ 56 +                 if isinstance(data, dict):                        │
  │ 57 +                     for key, value in data.items():               │
  │ 58 +                         if isinstance(value, str):                │
  │ 59 +                             result[key] = _parse_fraction(value)  │
  │ 60 +                         elif isinstance(value, (int, float)):     │
  │ 61 +                             # Avoid float if possible; but if gi… │
  │ 62 +                             result[key] = Fraction(str(value))    │
  │ 63 +             except Exception:                                     │
  │ 64 +                 # If YAML fails, fall back to defaults            │
  │ 65 +                 pass                                              │
  │ 66 +                                                                   │
  │ 67 +     if overrides:                                                 │
  │ 68 +         for override in overrides:                                │
  │ 69 +             if "=" not in override:                               │
  │ 70 +                 continue                                          │
  │ 71 +             key, value = override.split("=", 1)                   │
  │ 72 +             key = key.strip()                                     │
  │ 73 +             value = value.strip()                                 │
  │ 74 +             if key and value:                                     │
  │ 75 +                 result[key] = _parse_fraction(value)              │
  │ 76 +                                                                   │
  │ 77 +     return result                                                 │
  ╰────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────╯
